The user, on the iPhone release: "Preview button shouldnt show the Save or Cancel right? Just open up the browser? Else the save confirmation page that u just built is extra step towards the final output". Preview reverts to its tab on every device; only a save (Download) takes the share sheet, where the name matters, and File ready · Save is asked only when Safari refuses a share for a stale press. The same hour: the report head ran type, period, version, the portal's version, the brand, the logo and the sent day into one run-on line ("So many info here yet only in two sentences"); the meta line is now what the report is (type · period) and the rest are labelled facts under it (`.rp-facts`: Version, Client portal, This version where they match, Sent, For, Logo), two across in a narrow pane. And "the mark as sent card is missing?": a published report not yet sent showed Mark as sent only in the ⋯ and in the line after a PDF was drawn; the Sent fact is now a Mark as sent button on every step, and once sent the day itself with its pen, opening the same question as the ⋯.

### 2026-10-07 · A drawn PDF opens at an address that names it
The user, after Preview went back to its tab: "But the preview save to files needs to render the correct name ... Doesnt need the extra steps to render the correct name". Safari's viewer names a saved file after the last part of its address, and a `blob:` address has no name, so the console now hands each drawn file to its own service worker, which holds it for an hour in Cache Storage (`adspace-files`) and answers `/admin/file/{random id}/{file name}` with it (named in Content-Disposition as well). Preview opens that address; a save downloads from it; neither asks anything. This supersedes the share sheet for the console; the service worker's "caches nothing else" rule gains this one exception, never a script or a style. The client portal has no worker, so a client's iPhone save still takes the share sheet (File ready · Save only when Safari refuses a stale press). Not provable on an iPhone in the sandbox: Chromium in `tests/savename.js` asserts the address, the bytes, the header and the download's name.

### 2026-10-07 · Download on an iPhone opens the browser at the named address; the client portal too
The gate on the named-address change caught that Chrome sends an `<a download>` past the service worker, so a download link pointed at `/admin/file/` reached the server and failed; a download link now always carries the `blob:` (a desk keeps its name that way), and only a tab opens the worker's address. The user then: "Client view usually just focus on opening browser then go save to files if using iphone". On an iPhone or iPad every save opens a tab at the press (`ADspaceDocs.tabFor()`) and the file opens there under its name, so the share sheet and File ready · Save are left only for a blocked window. The client portal gains its own worker (`client/sw.js`, holding nothing but these files, through the shared `js/file-sw.js`), so a client's Download reads the same.

### 2026-10-07 · Drafts name the client by its brand, never 贵公司 or "your company"
The user: "for chinese version needs to avoid using 贵公司 / 貴司 all these very strong chinese wordings. use brand name to mention our client straight. also applies into english." The prompts had told the model the client is 贵品牌 or 您 (and "your" or the brand in English), because the client's name is masked before anything reaches Anthropic. The mask now reads `{brand}` rather than "the brand", the prompts tell the model to name the client by `{brand}` and never by a Chinese honorific or "your company", and the function fills `{brand}` with the name the report is for (a white-label report's brand, else the client) once the answer is back, so the name still never leaves. As a net, `brandIn` also turns 贵公司, 贵司, 贵品牌, 贵方, 贵企业 and 您公司 into the name. The check's quotes and fixes are filled the same way, so a quote still matches the text on the page.


### 2026-10-07 · Drafts never blame our own work, in words a partner would use
The user: "how would you make the creative agency for marketing and advertising reports analysis and evaluation claude drafting instructions? (cannot say things like Creative is bad, since we're the one providing creatives etc.)", then "go ahead". The rule already stood in one line (never call our work weak; read a shortfall as an audience finding); it now reads as a partner's report: a win credits the choice behind it (the offer, the hook, the format, the audience), a shortfall is written in three steps (the figure, what it shows about the audience or platform, what we will test), nobody is blamed (the client's product, price or reply time, the platform, the season) unless the team's notes give the reason, a fall is stated with its number and never dressed as a rise, and no result is promised. One list (`AVOID` in `report-draft`), in English and Chinese, names the words never used (weak, underperformed, failed, wasted, unfortunately, "only 3 leads"; 失败, 表现差, 不理想, 浪费, 遗憾…) with what to say instead (room to grow, a clear signal for the next test, 仍有提升空间, 有待优化…); the draft keeps to it and the figures check finds it, even in commentary typed by hand, giving the replacement as its fix, and flags a result blamed on the client's own business. Not replaced after the answer (as `brandIn` does for 贵公司): swapping a word without its sentence can change the meaning, so the colleague reads the check's finding and uses its fix.

### 2026-10-07 · Drafts are candid both ways, not only protective
The user, on the rules shipped the same hour: "the report could not also sound too protective for ourselves or way to beauty - eg. Totally no errors our services always perfect for continuously. It sounds fake and unreal as well as non realistic." The first pass had swung too far: it banned plain words such as underperformed and did not work, and offered comfort phrases in their place (room to grow, a clear signal for the next test, spend that showed us where to optimise next), which read as spin. The rule is now one of balance, so the good news is believed: the result is judged plainly and the work is never insulted. A shortfall is said with its figure, how far short and against what, and what we will change; a month weaker than the last is called weaker in the summary, not left to be found; a slip on our side that the notes name (a late launch, a broken link, an ad paused in error) is owned in one sentence with what was done; where the figures show no single cause the draft says so and names the test that will find out; a win is credited with its figure, never an adjective. `AVOID` now lists four kinds of words, English and Chinese: judgements of our work (bad, weak, boring, a mistake, wasted; 素材差, 失败, 浪费), praise no figure carries (excellent, outstanding, perfect, no issues, as always; 完美, 出色, 毫无问题, 零失误, 一如既往), comfort phrases in place of a figure (room to grow, valuable insights, a clear signal, positive momentum; 仍有提升空间, 宝贵经验), and words that colour a figure (only, unfortunately, thankfully; 仅仅, 遗憾, 幸好); plain words for a shortfall are given instead (lower, fell, fewer, cost more, did not reach, the least efficient; 下降, 低于, 未达到, 成本较高, 未达预期). The check gains a finding for a picture brighter than the figures: praise or certainty they do not carry, every ad said to have done well while one fell short, a fall told as good news, or a summary that reads as all good while a main figure got worse. The worked example now shows a weaker month called weaker, a shortfall stated against the strongest ad, and a cause the figures cannot show said so.

### 2026-10-07 · A UX audit of each section, run by one command, with a board
The user: "create mod to audit uiux and simplicity flows of each section". Three parts, so the method outlives the session and the board stays a convenience. (1) The method is a repo skill, `/ux-audit <section | all | changed>` (`.claude/skills/ux-audit/SKILL.md`), since a skill travels with the repo into every cloud session while a mod lives only where Claude Code draws panes: it reads the section's rules and the archive first (a choice the user made is never a finding; new evidence against one is "revisit"), measures, walks each key flow as its role (steps, questions, dead ends, recall, way back, repeats), judges through eight lenses in order, grades A to E on P1/P2/P3 counts, gives at most twelve findings with evidence and the smallest fix, and reports as an Artifact page, a short reply and the results file. It never changes portal code: fixes follow as a normal batch once the user picks them. (2) The measurement is `tests/uxsection.js`, a census rather than a pass or fail: per screen at 1280 (both themes for the console) and 390 under a finger, then every tab of the section's strip, the choices and how many sit in the first screen, blue and filled actions, fields, words and explanatory prose, screens of scroll, type sizes and ⋯ menus, each against a watch figure; targets, contrast and alignment stay uxaudit's and are not counted twice (a first draft counted small targets and flagged every legitimate 32px control). Each run is kept as `tests/ux-audit/<key>.json` so two audits compare in git. (3) The board is a Claude Code mod (`.claude/mods/ux-audit`, `/ux-board`), installable from this repo through `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`: each section's last grade and open findings from `tests/ux-audit/results.json`, "changed since" when git shows its scripts moved after the audit, and Audit buttons that run the skill. Its test caught that a mod may not submit a prompt beginning with `/`, so the buttons run the command (`$.command.run`). The results file lives in the private tests repo with the census, because the findings are the team's working notes; the board therefore needs the tests clone linked (§1 Setup) and reads Not audited without it.

### 2026-10-07 · My Records, Link expired, and the audit grades UI apart from UX
The user: "My HR sounds weird can change another term", and chose My Records from four offered (My records, My space, My file, Me); it matches the lock card the page already shows, Your records are locked. Only the words moved: the address stays `?s=mine`, the views and the functions keep their names, and nothing in the database said My HR. Its purpose line now names the health check-ins it holds. The same message: "short links that paused maybe use Link expired better than Link paused?". The visitor's page (the `adspace-links` Worker) now reads Link expired, the web's own phrase for a link that no longer opens; the console keeps Paused with Resume, because there the word names the team's act and its way back, and "expired" would say a date had passed when none was set (`tests/links.js` fails on the old wording). Then: "Ux done how about ui" and "Logical human interactions flow and design theories on ui". The audit already walked flows but left the interface to uxaudit's pass or fail; it now grades UX and UI apart. UX walks each flow with the four walkthrough questions at every step (will the person try it, notice the control, connect it to their goal, see what happened), Norman's two gulfs, the order of steps against what the person knows first, errors prevented before refused, the way back and the end (peak-end); UI reads every screen through ten lenses (hierarchy by squint, grid, proximity, consistency, type, colour per promise, spacing, affordance, states, both themes and the phone); every finding names the theory it breaks with its evidence (Fitts, Hick, Miller, Gestalt, Jakob, Von Restorff, Doherty, Tesler, progressive disclosure, serial position, peak-end, aesthetic-usability, WCAG, the golden ratio). The census gained the interface as the eye meets it: type weights, colours beside the neutrals (hue buckets), corner sizes, layout gaps off DESIGN.md's scale (a control's own icon gap left out, since 3px and 7px icon gaps flagged every screen), and the left edges of the first screen's blocks. The board shows UX and UI grades.

### 2026-10-07 · One colour, one meaning: every primary blue, a status a dot and its word
Phase 1 of the colour revamp the user approved after the portal-wide colour audit ("Ok good / Looking forward to phase 2 and 3 as soon as"), on Apple's own guidelines. The audit counted five reasons a screen turned colour (an ink primary for our own progress, a blue primary for a hand-off, green, amber and red pills for states, an amber slab for caution, a red slab for destruction) and found most screens a wall of tinted pills in which nothing said what to press. One meaning a colour now. **Blue (`#0071E3`, apple.com's) is the one primary action of a view**, Add, Create and Save as much as Publish, Release and Approve: `.btn-primary` and `.btn-go` are one style. This supersedes 2026-09-16 (blue only for what moves work to somebody else, the ink fill for our own operations) and 2026-09-28 (the rewards confirmations ink, not blue): two primaries a reader had to tell apart answered a question nobody asks, and the word on the button already says whether it hands the work on. `#0071E3` carries white at 4.70:1; its hover and press are darker (`#0068D1`, `#005BB8`) because Apple's lighter hover falls to 4.32:1; `#007AFF` stays refused at 4.02:1; dark keeps the same blue under white, as apple.com does, and blue words take `--action-ink` (`#0066CC`, `#2997FF` in dark). **A status is a dot and its word in ink** (App Store Connect's own style): `.chip-state` and every toned chip lose their tinted ground, keep the word at 12.5/500 and take an 8px dot in Apple's system colours (`--dot-ok`, `--dot-wait`, `--dot-late`, `--dot-off`): green done or live, amber waiting on somebody or still running, red needs acting on now, grey not started, paused or closed. A stage select carries the dot inside its own box (`--sdot`), never a tint. A fact (High, Goodwill, White label, You) is the bare grey tag, never a status colour: it had been warn or green where it described rather than reported. **Three button roles, as Apple's**: the blue primary; the tonal button for everything else, caution included (`.btn-warn` is tonal now: Unpublish, Withdraw, Stop, Revert and Reopen cost nothing to look at, and the question each opens says what it costs); `.btn-danger` red words on the tonal button, never a red slab, which read as an alarm. **The stage tones follow the law**: ready to start is grey (not started), in progress and a revision amber (still running), waiting amber, cleared green, blocked red; Waiting on client, In production and the everyday In progress and Review the same. **What stays**: red for late and urgent and amber for waiting (2026-10-07, above) as dots; the announcement's Important and the upgrade line as the neutral band led by the amber dot rather than the warn pair; a tab's count red only where what it counts needs acting on now, a waiting or done count neutral; `.msg.warn` the words in ink after an amber dot; Health's pink and the calendar's purple in their one place each; chart bars over a guidance figure in `--warn`. The DESIGN.md register line "no coloured dots for status" is replaced: the dot is not decoration but the one colour a status keeps, always beside its word. Phases 2 (sizes that flow) and 3 (calm and elegant) follow as their own batches.

### 2026-10-07 · Several announcements at once, in one sliding bar, and Delete
The user, an hour after announcements shipped: "Omg i couldnt create two announcements at once? As i said earlier i want to have multiples", then "Your announcement portal doesnt look like it is for one only. But i want multiples for internal and also clients / The announcement bar showing side should not stack else it will push down all contents. It should be maybe some obvious sliding 1/2 2/2 or 1/3 2/3 3/3". One at a time (the entry above, "one saved ends every other of its audience") is superseded by `2026-10-07-announcements-several.sql`: `announcement_now` answers every live one as a list (Important first, then the newest, ten at most; the page still accepts a single object from before the migration runs), and neither `announcement_save` nor `announcement_end` (Restore) stops another. The page keeps one bar: a track of slides, one a line, moved by ‹ and › with `1/3` between them (round from the last to the first) and by a swipe on a phone (`data-swipe-prev` / `-next`); every slide is laid in the same box, so the bar is as tall as its longest line and moving along never moves the page under it. A reader's × hides the one on show and leaves the rest. A stack was refused because it pushes every page's content down by a line a notice. From the screenshot "Why cannot delete?" (a stopped announcement offered Restore alone): `announcement_delete(p_id)` deletes one that is stopped or whose end has passed, refused `live` until then (Stop comes first, so nothing on a reader's screen disappears without the team seeing it go), asked first with no restore, and filed `team.changed` under Announcements; the list shows every line of each audience, live ones with Edit and Stop, stopped or ended ones with Restore (while their end is ahead) and Delete. The function holds the word `delete`, so it is handed to the SQL Editor (§3). `tests/announce.js` and `tests/smsql.js` §9x2 hold it.

### 2026-10-07 · Every typed box grows, on every page, from one script
The user, typing an announcement on an iPhone: "Cell not increasing as i typed? Why still these issues again". The review page's request box (2026-10-04) and a performance review's body had each been given their own grow, and every other textarea kept the height it was drawn at, so the third line was written out of sight. The fix is one mechanism, not a third page-local listener: `js/grow.js`, loaded on every page that holds a typed box (the console, the client portal, the selection page, the creator's page, the review page, the page template), fits each textarea as it is typed in (`input` and `focusin`, captured), as it comes into view holding words (an `IntersectionObserver` fed by a `MutationObserver`, since a box inside a hidden sheet measures nothing until the sheet opens), when a page sets its value (the `value` setter wrapped, then a fit once drawn), and when the width changes. It stops at 60% of the screen (`visualViewport`, so an iPhone's keyboard is counted) and scrolls past it, and while it measures, every scroller around the box and the page itself are put back, so the sheet never jumps under the reader. `data-nogrow` keeps a box still. The two local copies now call `ADspaceGrow.fit`. `tests/grow.js` types, empties, overfills and reopens a saved value on the console's question sheet and types on the review page, at 1280 and at 390 under a finger; with `js/grow.js` blanked eight of its twelve checks fail, so it holds the mechanism rather than a sentence.

### 2026-10-07 · A status keeps its words together, and every stage name fits its select
The gate on the colour change caught two of its own faults. A status chip had become a flex box so its dot could sit on the word's baseline; a word holding a part of its own (a post's `Changes <span>requested</span>`, whose second word gives way on a narrow pane) then split into two flex items a 6px gap apart, which a reader sees as a wide space and a screen reader's text reads as two lines (`tests/revise.js`). The dot is now an empty inline box, whose foot is its baseline, so it sits where it sat and the word stays one phrase: measured old against new, every chip keeps its width, height, baseline and the word's start, and only Changes requested narrows by the gap it had gained. The stage select's word had moved 15px right to make room for its dot, and My Work's longest stages (Performance review, Changes requested) no longer read whole in the 160px column (`tests/work.js`); the dot now stands 8px in and the word 6px after it, as on a chip, which gives the room back without moving a column. The client record's stage select, which stands alone in the head, is as wide as its longest stage (Proposal sent was cut by under a pixel at 124px), with 124px its floor; no suite measures a select's text, so it was measured by hand at 1280 and 390. And the front door's Visit website was drawn in the button blue on the light grey band (4.31:1, `uxaudit` contrast): blue as words or a glyph is `--action-ink`, as the token already said, so the six places that drew text or a glyph in `--action` (the front door's link, a board card's hovered title, the record's saving tick, the timeline's, the new route's ⓘ, the purpose card's mark) now take it.

### 2026-10-08 · Long words wrap, the handle only makes a box taller, and every delete has its level
The user: "audit the delete for every created thing, and also the input cells whereby some will have long text it should expand and cells couldnt be movable", then, of the corner handle, "it should compromise the viewbox and couldnt be drag around like a toy the purpose is to just expand the textbox input longer larger". **Long words.** A one-line field that may hold more than its width (a title, a link, a next action, a reason, a name typed back) slid its words out of sight; each is now a textarea marked `data-oneline`, a field's height whose words wrap onto more lines, with no handle, no line break (a pasted one becomes a space) and Enter submitting as a field's would. Twenty-six console fields, the creator's links, the booking's notes and post link, Documents' entries, a piece's description, Performance's reasons and Reports' account, post and ad fields took it; the question sheet asks words in one (a count typed back or the next task number stays a field with the number keyboard, `numeric()`), and `ADspaceAsk.rename` opens a title in one, hung from the top of its line so the line keeps its height (a textarea standing on the baseline added the descent: 25px became 32). A date, a time, a number or a code stays an input. **The handle.** `resize: vertical`, its `min-height` the fitted height (so it never hides the words) and its `max-height` 85% of the screen; a height dragged to is kept while typing (`data-floor`) and forgotten when the page sets new words. **Deletes.** Every created thing was walked for its way out. Six tables carried one policy for every action, reading at View, and Postgres checks a delete against USING alone, so a colleague at View could delete a client's service lines, letters and requests and a draft report's accounts, posts and ads through the API: each is now four policies (delete at Manage, or at Work for a draft report's rows, which the report's steps remove), proved in a database of its own against a real ladder of levels, the old policies laid first to show the hole (`2026-10-08-delete-at-its-level.sql`, `tests/sql.js` §DL). A removed call or visit gains Delete at Clients: Calls Full Access, as a contact has; a removed service line, which vanished once its Undo passed, is listed on asking with Restore and never deleted (a letter may have quoted it); a removed contact's Delete no longer removes it again first (it filed a second removal and offered an Undo that brought the contact back); restore and delete are filed as themselves (`2026-10-08-activity-files-restore-and-delete.sql`). Enter in the question sheet no longer sends while an input method is composing. Put to the user rather than decided: withdrawing a performance query before it is answered, deleting a colleague's own health check-in, deleting a withdrawn initiative, and deleting a document type, a white-label brand, a template family or a handbook version that nothing names.

### 2026-10-08 · The client pages refresh: one icon set, the campaign room, the creator's next step, the portal's meeting, and every step named
The user approved the proposal ("Proceed the revamped client pages proposal") for the three client pages. **Icons.** `js/icons.js` holds one outline set at the portal's 15px and 1.8 stroke, in ink, for fact labels, chips and the drop area. **Campaign room** (`/creators/`). The name card says where the campaign stands (n of N posted, a segment a booking in its state's dot, a legend, the next shoot); a draft waiting on the client leads the page as its own card with the decision on it; the results lead with views, then impressions, engagements, engagement rate and per engagement, with the top post once two are live. Creator photos were tried and refused: "The creator needs numbering not profile photos" (initials of Chinese names also overflowed); the rows keep their numbers. Do you think Apple would put such huge stage status". ### 2026-10-08 · Twelve fixes from one list: lists open at the top, Paused folds, report tasks follow their report, a noon reminder, a bell that keeps a week
The user's list of bugs and questions. **Lists open low.** Every list opened a little lower on each visit: bars drawn above a list while it loaded pushed the page down, the push was saved as the person's place and restored next time. A list now opens at its top on a visit or refresh, only Back and Forward restore its place, and nothing is saved until the person scrolls; a client record still restores its own scroll on refresh. **Paused** is its own band between Clients and Past clients (Documents' client picker follows the bands), and the reason for Paused or Past, with its note, is changed later from the Timeline's pen, the stage log entry rewritten and filed from and to. **Report tasks follow their report** (trigger `sm_reports_task_follow`): made, In progress under its maker; in review, confirmed or published unsent, Waiting; sent, Review; Done stays the owner's press, a finished task never moves, and a white-label brand's report leaves the month's task alone, since one month holds one task of each kind. **A noon reminder** on weekdays (12:00 MYT) tells a colleague who is not an admin when no open task is assigned to them, or when they have never added one; "never" was chosen over "not this week" so a colleague busy on assigned work is not nagged daily, and no new figure was introduced. **The bell** lists unread first and then Earlier, the last seven days of read notices (ten at most): marking read no longer empties it. **Calendar** chips are two lines at 11px and a day shows five before `+N more`. **Stars** fill from the first to the one under the pointer. **HR types** spell the whole reference, `ADHR/{Employee ID}/{code}{YYMM}`, in place of "Code E". **In-place style**: the task record's Details selects are drawn as the value (no box, the tonal ground on hover), the Month row no longer restates the month, and the Content Review settings put the logo address on a row of its own. **My records** is sentence case. Two items needed no build: Announcements already exist (account menu, Announcements, `team.announce`), and creator drafts become private once CloudFront's `content/*` behaviour requires signed cookies (`docs/S3-STORAGE.md` §6b, the user's step; the pages already carry the media pass).

Chosen: `team_members.system`, set only from the database, with a trigger that lets only the account itself change its standing, date, group, admin mark or email; the console leaves it out of every picker from one read beside `me()`; Team tags it System with no ⋯ for others. Which address is marked is data applied through the connector, never committed. **Deciding as.** A client's typed name was kept on the device with no way to see or change it, so a typo stayed until the browser was cleared. One line under the name card names it with Change and Forget (like the creator's Forget this device); past decisions keep their name, since a decision is a record. A line per card was rejected as noise on a page of many posts. **Media pass on the client portal.** Before CloudFront requires the pass on `content/*` (S3-STORAGE §6b), the portal's logos would have failed: the portal never asked for a pass and `media-pass` answered colleagues alone. `media-pass` now also answers a signed-in client contact with live portal access, and the portal asks before it draws. PDFs were checked and are unaffected: the wide report logo is stored as a data URL.

The `meta-import` function answers Meta's figures as the very exports the importers already read (`shape.mjs`), so there is one importer, one matching by Ad ID or link, one summary line and one filing: an export written by the function and pasted by a person cannot read differently. Facebook post views are `post_media_view` (Meta retired `post_impressions` on 15 Nov 2025) and each metric is asked on its own, so one retired metric drops one column rather than the import. Deployed with Verify JWT off, against the brief's "on": every console function runs that way because the gateway refuses the CORS preflight (recorded 2026-09-24 and 2026-09-30), and the function asks the database about the caller itself. Live Meta calls are not exercised by any suite (Meta is unreachable from the sandbox and no token exists yet); `tests/metashape.js` drives the mapping from Graph-shaped fixtures and `tests/metaimport.js` the console end to end through the stand-in.

### 2026-10-08 · Write caption in Content Review, and what the AI costs
The user approved the caption writer ("1 2 ok") and asked for real costing ("Also check costing"). Write caption sits under a post's caption fields in Add assets and in a saved post's Edit, at Content Review: Sets at Work. It asks first, because a call is paid for: the main language (English or Bahasa Melayu, from the main contact), 中文 (ticked where the post or the set already holds Chinese), XHS Safe Mode on a rednote post only and unticked, since the user applies it only once confirmed, and notes, kept in this browser like the report's draft notes, else the brief of a task naming the set. The `caption-draft` function is built as `report-draft` is: it checks the caller under their own access, claims the use in the database before Claude is asked, marks it done or failed, and never sends an image, a contact or the client's name (the name and handles are masked as {brand} and {handle} and filled on the page). The words land in the existing caption fields and are saved only by the post's own Save, so a caption is reviewed before anything changes; Undo puts the earlier words back. The house style is the user's own copy standard: benefit first, a hook, an emoji and bold title where it suits, one CTA, no dashes, Malaysia and Singapore context, Chinese written as Chinese, each platform's own voice, brand names exactly as written.
Captions are counted apart (purpose `caption`, 20 a colleague and 40 an admin a day, settings) so writing captions never spends a report's draft or check. For costing, every call now keeps its input and output tokens and model on its row, from both functions, failed calls included because they are billed; the AI usage page prices this month at Business settings `ai_price_in` 4 and `ai_price_out` 20 (US$ a million tokens, Opus 5.5's list price), each row at the prices of its own day, so a later price change never rewrites the past. Uses from before tokens were kept are counted and named as not priced, never guessed. The brief asked for Verify JWT on; the function is deployed with it off, as every browser-called function here is, because the preflight carries no Authorization header and the function asks the database about the caller itself.

### 2026-10-09 · The Report audit: every figure the report takes from Meta, exactly
The user asked for the figures check to be renamed for what it does and for a check against Meta beside it, then: "there shouldnt be any mismatches". The card under Check and submit is now the Report audit in two parts. The Commentary is the AI's reading of the words, as before. Against Meta uses no AI: the page reads Meta through `meta-import` and runs the answer through the importer's own plan (the ads plan was lifted out of the paste sheet, `adsPlan`), so a difference is exactly what an import would change and there is still one importer and one way of matching. No tolerance setting was made, against the first proposal of 1%: every figure must equal Meta's at the precision the report holds it, and Submit and Publish wait on a match in the page and in the database (`meta-audit`); no reason or role passes one. Each row puts itself right (Use Meta's figure, Add, Remove), Update all from Meta does every figure and missing row, and an ad or post Meta does not hold is only removed on its own press, since it may carry an image or remarks the team made. Each reading is filed with a print of the figures taken by the database, so a figure typed after the reading holds the report again; the page compares, the database decides what still matches. Meta revises a period's figures for weeks (late conversions, reach recounted, invalid activity removed), so an Advertising Report is read again at the press of Publish and needs a match since it was confirmed; if Meta moved, Send back with Meta's changes returns it to draft with the rows as its note, and the submitter takes Meta's figures in one press and submits it to the reviewer again. Chosen over a function that would write figures into a confirmed report: a second write path past the draft guard for one case. An Accounts Report is different: Meta gives a post's figures only as lifetime totals that grow by the hour, so no second reading can ever match the first; it is read in draft, and Publish holds the reading it was submitted on, its time stamped (Matches Meta as of …). A report submitted before the audit holds no reading and is not held. Meta not answering is filed and holds the report; an admin alone continues without it, with a reason, so an outage on deadline day stops nothing it should not. A client with no links is not held and reads Not linked with Link accounts. Followers are typed by the team and outside the audit: Meta answers a Page's followers as they are today, never as they were on a past day. A post's link is matched as one key (Instagram by its shortcode; else host and path without www., a trailing slash or a tracking query), which the import now uses too, so a post copied with `?igsh=` is never added twice. The words: Report audit, Against Meta, Commentary, Run audit; the record files `report.audited` as Report audit, and a commentary check as Report audit · Commentary; older rows keep Figures check. `tests/audit.js` walks it in the console; `tests/smsql.js` §9zc holds the gate.

Every page had carried the pass since 2026-10-03, the client portal since 2026-10-08, and `media-pass` was answering them with the signing key held. A chip is now two blocks of one line each, cut to the cell: the client (the stage dot before it; Internal or Lead · name where it applies) over the code and the format's short word (Static, Carousel, Reel 30s, so `2609W401 · Static` fits a desk cell where `Graphic: Static` was cut), a task with no code its name. Separate blocks were chosen over the clamp because a single nowrap line clamps in every browser. ### 2026-10-09 · A report on request, and Meta's idle ads left out
**On request.** "Sometimes we need to create report on demand. Like situation like now client requests a report." The month gate made from the same day held every report to a month in My Work that asks for it, and the month's report task followed whatever report ended in its span, so a two-week report for a client would have been refused at Work, or, made by an admin, would have moved the month's task and counted as the month's report. A report now records whether the client asked for it (`sm_reports.on_request`, set once at creation): New report's Requested by the client tick opens every month, and a custom period is always one, since the month's report is the month's own span. Such a report is held to no month (no gate, no month rows under Check and submit, `sm_report_gate` applies false), never moves or resets the month's task, and is never the month's report in New report's months, the Overview's reports owed or the task card's report row. A month's report carries on from the month's report before it, never from one asked for; an Advertising Report asked for compares with the same length just before it, typed by the team, since a month's figures beside two weeks mislead. The four-argument `sm_report_create` stays the month's; `sm_report_create_for` takes `p_on_request`, its old signature dropped first. The stand-in answered New report's months as not found for every client (its handler sat after the report lookup), so no suite had ever seen a greyed month; it now answers as the database does and `tests/reports.js` measures the greying and the tick.
**Meta's idle ads.** "it automatically imports those with 0 spend or 0 results ... when going into the audit ... it blocks". Meta's ad insights answer every ad with any delivery in the period, including impressions with no billable spend and ads that spent with no result; the team removed them, and the audit then asked for each again as an ad the report lacks, so the report could not be submitted. The user chose the rule (2026-10-09, of three offered): Import from Meta and the Report audit both leave out an ad with no spend or no results; an ad the report already holds is still read against Meta, so a row kept on purpose is checked, never asked to be removed. The trade-off stated with the choice: an objective's spend in the report counts only ads with results, while Step 1 keeps Meta's whole account spend. Import from Ads Manager (a file the team exported) is unchanged.

And any windows that pops up from the bottom part would come up above the tab bar", then: "fits for iphone duo on the right once launched too right?" (the foldable iPhone). Recommended and agreed ("Ok") with one change to the second half: a card that pops up (a ⋯ menu, the bell, the account menu, the ⓘ card, a guide, More, the confirm bar) keeps above the bar, but a sheet covers it, as an iPhone app's sheet does, since a form with the bar live under a thumb invites leaving unsaved work and loses 80px of its height. The sections were behind ≡ at the top left, the hardest place a thumb reaches. The bar holds the first four sections the person may open, in the rail's order (the rail is already ordered by how often each is opened), and More; five or fewer with no Activity record take it whole. More lists only the sections the bar does not, against the first idea of the full list, because the same section offered twice on one screen is what the duplicate-action audit removed that morning. The chosen tab is the rail's own chosen fill behind the glyph, not the screenshot's blue, which is the action's colour here. One media query decides it, `(max-width: 640px), (pointer: coarse) and (max-width: 1023px)`, held once in the head script so the bar's room and the head without ≡ are drawn from first paint, and followed live, so a foldable gets the bar folded and open, an upright tablet gets it, and a desk window keeps the rail (the drawer stays for a narrow desk window). The bar steps away while a field takes the keyboard under a finger. `tests/tabbar.js` measures it; the walks press its tabs and More where they pressed ≡.

### 2026-10-09 · A button's glyph takes the button's ink
"My blue button with plus icon is still blue background and grey plus icon", then a screenshot of Issue letter and "most of the blue buttons are like this in light mode". The record heads' rule for their own mark (`.viewhead svg`, mute ink at 18px, and `.panelhead svg`) reached every button placed in the head, so on a client's record Add contact, Add service, Issue letter, Issue document, New campaign and Open Content Review drew a grey glyph on blue, and Billing's and Brand's Edit a faded one. ### 2026-10-09 · A list reads its rows in one request
Supabase's usage page showed log ingestion at 0.73 GB of the 1 GB included ("why is it using so much"). Read live over a day: about 41 MB, two thirds of it API requests (every request is a logged line of about 2.8 KB), a quarter Supabase's own connection checks. The largest single source was the Content Review list: it read each client's sets with a request of its own, and painted again on every key typed in its search, 1,497 requests a day, a sixth of the API's. It now reads every listed client's sets in one request (`.in()`, paged by the thousand) once a visit, and a repaint reads nothing. The once-a-minute checks (the bell, upgrade mode, announcements: 2,063 requests a day) were put to the user as merging them into one request, or one every two minutes.

### 2026-10-09 · An open page's minute is one request
The user approved merging the minute checks. An open console asked four times a minute (upgrade mode, the announcements, the bell's unread and its earlier notices) and a client page twice: about a seventh of all logged requests. `page_pulse(p_audience, p_bell)` answers the three in one, each part by its own function's rules (the team's announcements to the team, the bell to its reader alone), and every page joins its parts to one timer in `js/maintenance.js` (`often(key, take, alone, opts)`). A database without the function answers PGRST202, and each part then asks alone, so the pages run before the migration does. Loading a page still reads each part once; only the minute is merged. The cadence stays one minute: the two-minute option was offered and not taken.

### 2026-10-09 · A copy that never ran is never the ad's figures
The user's Report audit read 16 differences on one ad (2608W4_WhyChoose · Leads): the result type Leads against Meta's "reach", and every figure against 0. Meta's export held two copies of the creative under one name and objective: the one that delivered, and one that never ran, whose result type falls back to Meta's default (reach) with zeros. Both matched the same row, and the idle copy, read second, wrote over the first. The plan now reads the copies that delivered first, and a copy with no spend or no results never writes over an ad a delivering copy already fed; it is counted as left out, as an idle ad is. The audit runs the same plan, so it reads the delivering copy alone.

### 2026-10-09 · A batch never waits on every suite
The user: "your test is now taking from 15 mins to 90 mins which is ridiculous." The full gate had grown to about 120 suites and ran on every batch touching a shared script. A batch now runs the suites that call what changed (found with `grep -l` over `tests/`) plus `ui`; the full gate runs once a day after the merges, and what it finds is the next batch.

### 2026-10-09 · On-site meetings: recorded after the day, and the outstation reminder
The user asked for a physical meeting beside the online one and No meeting this month. It already existed as On site under Channel, so the user's answer set the work: "on-site please allow add past date. and add one more reminder for on-site to submit the outstation record (go.adspace.me/outstation)". An online meeting is still put in the diary from the next half hour, because it books a calendar and messages the client; an on-site one is often written down after the visit, so the sheet and `ops_engagement_set_meeting` accept a past date for it, and it asks for no link. Once an on-site meeting has ended, a cron every fifteen minutes tells whoever led it (else the month's manager) once to submit the outstation record; a meeting moved to another time is told again. The notice carries the form's address in a new `ops_notifications.link` column (https only), and the bell and a push open the form itself rather than a console page, because the act asked for is the form. The address lives in the reminder function alone (a URL is not a business figure, and `app_settings` holds numbers). On-site meetings that ended before the reminder began were counted as told, so the first run sent no backlog.

### 2026-10-09 · The Report audit runs on its button alone, against the figures as they stand
The user, on the Report audit reading Reading Meta… again: "THE AUDIT KEEPS AUTO RUN, IT SHOULD ONLY TRIGGER AFTER ENTERING THE BUTTON", and "i dont know why its imported but the audit still have some variances of few cents". The live record of one Advertising Report showed why both were one fault: three colleagues had it open, and each page read Meta by itself when Check and submit opened (every ten minutes), compared Meta with the figures the page had loaded, and filed the result. One colleague took Meta's figures (a match at 09:49:21); eleven seconds later a page opened before that compared its old figures (impressions one short, a cent of spend, rates as they stood) and filed a mismatch, which held the report again. Against Meta now runs only on Run audit (and an Advertising Report at Publish, as before), and every reading first reads the report's figures again from the database; when they moved, the page is drawn again as they now stand. Opening the step reads the last reading filed, or Not run.

The user: "Video Script should be a new section to generate the PDF … a script table covers one video, hence there is a # … choose which type of the video script, then add the details, add each scene where needed, remarks/notes section; then the Video # is same as V1 V2 V3 … shortcut create V2 with the same header … three different types of script, detailed scenes / products + scenes / story + VO. VC# is correct the video clip number on camera … one digital similar as content review able to view online (professional format, with interactions tick or whatsoever u suggest build); another is export pdf for on-site use." The Google Drive export of the template was not to be followed for its format; its structure (the header table's eight facts, each kind's blocks, VC# beside each, Notes / Remarks, the wordmark at the head and Private & Confidential with the page at the foot) was. Built as a section of its own on the ladder, each group starting at its Content Review level: one script a video, the next video of a shoot copying the header, the client deciding online under a typed name as on the review page, the crew's clip number and Shot tick kept in the portal (never shown to the client) and printed on the PDF, an empty VC# cell left for the pen. A change to what the client decided on after their decision is the next round, as a post's is. Write script (AI) is the next step, not in this one.

The first build was lost with the container on the morning of 2026-10-09 (it had not been pushed); it was rebuilt from the database suite that survived (`tests/vssql.js`) and the session's record, and a working branch is now pushed as it grows.

### 2026-10-09 · My Work's stage column is 170px

The track is measured against its worst face, so it is 170px at a desk and on a narrow pane; under a finger it stays 180px, which already held 13.5px. Smaller padding was weighed and refused: the dot and word keep the status chip's own spacing everywhere.

A video script now does the same (`.rec-linktools`, the campaign's own rules): the client's address, Copy link, Preview, then Share with client in blue or Unshare, which asks; Unshare left the ⋯, which keeps Edit, Reset client link and Delete. The key is the client's (one for all its videos), read once a client; a colleague at Work makes it the first time, so the address shows before the first share as a campaign's does before it is published. No Live tone beside it: the head's state already says With client.

### 2026-10-09 · Write script
The user: "go ahead and write script". Built as Write caption is, for a video: a press on the editor's Script heading asks the video length (15, 30, 60 or 120 seconds), the notes and, on a rednote script alone, XHS Safe Mode; `script-draft` claims one of the colleague's scripts for the day (10, an admin 20, apart from the reports' and the captions' counts), reads the script and client as the caller, and asks Claude for the type's own fields by structured output, the client's name and handles masked and no cast member named (only how many). The words go into the fields with Undo; Save keeps them. The reports' day limits now count drafts and checks by name (`purpose in ('draft', 'check')`), so no later AI use takes from them by accident.

### 2026-10-09 · Video scripts by content month, edited on a page, read on the link
The user, on the first build: scripts numbered V1, V2 by shoot did not fit a team planning by content month; "the default for video script can be YYMMVSNN"; the editor as a sheet risked a long script on a stray press ("make it like a page"); the key facts belonged apart from the scenes; one script is one full video; and the client link is for reading the script and recording VC# on site, not for approving it. Built so: `period`, `seq` and a stored `code`, unique a client a month, the lowest number free; earlier scripts took their shooting month, else the month they were made. The editor is a page of its own whose changes are never dropped by navigation: Cancel asks, and Back, the rail or a return to the section bring it back as it was. The link page lost its stage strip and decisions; `script_decide` answers `closed` rather than being dropped, so no grant or hygiene list moves for it, and `script_shot_link` joins `open_to_anon` for the on-site clip numbers. The script type is a select, not a segment: Products and scenes did not fit a phone's third.

### 2026-10-09 · WhatsApp through the Cloud API
The user: "Have you built the whatsapp send? … What i mean is the whatsapp api", then chose Report to client, Team reminders, Creator updates and "Manually send with button a feedback approved template on whatsapp". The WhatsApp Business ID and phone number the user said they had sent were not in any record this session could reach (nor in the database), so the sender reads `WHATSAPP_PHONE_ID` and `WHATSAPP_TOKEN` from Supabase's secrets and the screens stay quiet until a template is set. A business-initiated WhatsApp message must use a template Meta approved, so every purpose names its template in Business settings, with how many variables it takes, rather than words typed in the code. Reports and the feedback request are sent at the press as the colleague (the report's kept PDF uploaded as the header document, then marked as sent); reminders and creator steps are queued by trigger beside the push ones (separate triggers, so nothing existing was redefined) and sent with the service role, tried three times. Creators had no phone number at all; a WhatsApp number joined their record. The 6 Oct decision not to build a Share button stands: that was about handing a file to a chosen group from the browser, which WhatsApp's links cannot do.

### 2026-10-10 · The WhatsApp approval reminder
The user said yes to the chaser proposed after the WhatsApp section: a
reminder to a client whose content set or creator draft has waited on their
approval. It is sent by hand, never by itself, because a reminder the team did
not mean to send costs the client relationship more than a late approval
does. It sits in My Work's Waiting for you, on the clients a colleague holds
and the campaigns they run, where the person who chases already looks:
Awaiting approval, with Remind. A set waits from its publishing and a draft
from its last quality check (the release to the client), each once it has
waited `approval_reminder_days`, a Business setting (3) in Due dates, as
every figure the team may change is. Remind opens the one composer with
what waits and the link to approve it filled in; the database checks the
record named is the contact's client's and is still waiting, and files the
message against it. It is a purpose of its own with its own part, so a
group may chase clients without sending anything else. The purpose check
on `wa_templates` had to be replaced, which the Supabase connector will not
run, so that one statement is its own file for the SQL Editor; the settings
key list is now checked against every seeded setting (`tests/sql.js`),
because another branch restates `app_settings_set` the same day. The
Overview was left as it is: its Sets waiting on the client card already
lists the sets, and a second place to send from would be the same act
twice.

### 2026-10-10 · WhatsApp, a section of its own (Phase 1)
Approved by the user as WhatsApp Phase 1; the inbox and messages to
colleagues (Phase 2) were left out. WhatsApp had grown three buttons on
three records, three parts on three sections and a sheet in the account
menu, with no answer to "did it arrive?". It is now a section after Clients
(whose contacts it mostly messages): Messages, every message the portal
sent with what Meta reports of it, and at Full Access Templates. Its levels
read as every section's: View reads, Manage sends, Full Access sets the
templates. The three sending parts moved under it and narrow it (follow or
shut, as the My Work views), because a send is now one act whatever record
it starts from; a send also asks the record's own section at View, so the
section never opens a record its group cannot read. Groups started from
their Clients level, Admin at Full Access, as asked. The composer is one
sheet every record opens prefilled: To is a client contact or a creator,
never a colleague; the template is chosen from Meta's approved list read
through `wa-send` (`WHATSAPP_WABA_ID`), never typed, because a name typed
wrong was the commonest refusal; each variable is prefilled and editable and
the message is previewed as it will read; Send names the recipient, the
number and the category Meta charges it as. The function asks the database
for the number and reads the template again from Meta at the press, so
nothing the page sends decides who receives what. Delivery comes from Meta's
webhook (`wa-hook`), believed only with its signature over the raw body, and
recorded against the message id the sender kept, by a function only the
service role runs. A message delivered or read never turns failed. The old
by-hand functions stay for a page loaded before the change. `wa.sent` moved
from Clients to WhatsApp in the activity record; `campaign.whatsapp` stays
for the booking resends queued before. The activity map went into a file of
its own because it names tags the Supabase connector stops on.

### 2026-10-09 · Every new content piece carries its three dates
The user: "could you force to have required first draft date, due date and
the post date (as required) to curb delays?" On 2026-10-05 and 06 the three
dates were typed per line and a blank one stayed blank; the post date was
"tentative and never required". From now a piece is made only with all
three: the New sheet marks them required and refuses a line missing one,
naming the post, and `ops_create_pieces` refuses the same
(`dates-required`, with the piece's place), so no other caller can skip
them. Tasks made before keep their dates as they are. Add task (an everyday
task), templates, copies, repeats and a month's report tasks are untouched:
a report task has one date by the user's choice of 2026-10-05, and a repeat
counts from its first piece, which now always has a post date.


### 2026-10-10 · Words written by AI are declared read before they are saved
The user, 2026-10-09: "Write caption, Write draft is not clear that this is
going to be written by AI", and "a warning dialog for all AI assisted write
out (if its manually written 100% then no need) ... they declare that the
contents are read and confirmed ... Should there have any critical issues it
could affect your performance". Every writer now reads Write with AI with
the AI mark before it, Writing while it runs, and its Undo line says Written
by AI. Read before saving (submitting, on a report). Saving a caption or a
script whose words came from Write with AI, or submitting a report whose
commentary did, asks once, in the same sheet as the save or the submit: the
words were written with AI, read in full and checked for facts, figures,
names, prices and claims; the person who confirms is responsible, and an
error may be raised as an issue in their performance review; a tick that
refuses until ticked. Words typed by hand ask nothing. A report's answer
comes from the database (`ai_written`), so a reload never loses it; a
caption's and a script's from the page, which holds the words until they are
saved. The declaration is filed with the save under the act's own tag, so
the Activity record map did not change.

getting longer", and AI usage "hidden under Reports while the AI usage

crew reads one sheet and ticks it by pen: every scene now has a Shot box
beside its VC# cell (ticked and numbered where the console recorded it), the
words said are set heavier than what is seen so the talent finds their line,

carries no VC#, which it never had a use for.

### 2026-10-10 · Views, restrictions and interest, answered inside the work
The user asked how the portal could help with low views, restricted
accounts, content that is not interesting and delays. Four answers, each
inside a step the team already takes rather than a new screen: Reels and
Graphics checklists gain checks against the commonest causes of low reach
and restrictions (the hook, licensed music, original footage, no absolute
claims); a client's content brief on its Brand pane (audience, pain points,
pillars, tone, what to avoid, competitors, hooks that worked) is read by
both AI writers; a post gone live records its views and engagements a week
later, and a month being planned lists last month's best and weakest posts;
a restricted account is marked on the client, red in its rail and listed on
the Overview. Delays were answered on 2026-10-09 by the three required
dates; the WhatsApp approval chaser waits on the WhatsApp section.
